Options:

                        level
  -o LOGDIR, --logdir=LOGDIR

                        ./logs
  -s SERVER, --server=SERVER

                        192.168.1.83:9123
  -l LISTEN, --listen=LISTEN
                        accept OSC client from DAW at host:port. default
                        192.168.1.83:9124
  -c CONNECT, --connect=CONNECT
                        Connect to DAW OSC server at host:port. default
                        192.168.1.83:9125
